SELECT `w`.`wishlist_id` AS `WishlistId`, `p`.`product_id` AS `ProductId`, `p`.`product_name` AS `ProductName`, `w`.`sku_id` AS `SkuId`, `p`.`buscat_id` AS `Buscatid`, `p`.`prod_subcat_id` AS `prod_sub_catid`, COALESCE(`p1`.`file_name`, `p3`.`file_name`) AS `VariantImageUrl`, COALESCE(`s`.`discount_price`, `s`.`sku_cost`) AS `Price`, `b`.`bus_reg_id` AS `StoreId`, `b`.`business_name` AS `StoreName`, (`p`.`product_status` = 'Approved') AND `p`.`is_active` AS `IsProductAvailable`
FROM `wishlist` AS `w`
INNER JOIN `products` AS `p` ON `w`.`product_id` = `p`.`product_id`
INNER JOIN `business_registers` AS `b` ON `w`.`bus_reg_id` = `b`.`bus_reg_id`
INNER JOIN `sku_product_variants` AS `s` ON `w`.`sku_id` = `s`.`sku_id`
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT `p0`.`file_name`, `p0`.`sku_id`
    FROM `product_images` AS `p0`
    WHERE `p0`.`sort_order` = 1
) AS `p1` ON `w`.`sku_id` = `p1`.`sku_id`
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT `p2`.`file_name`, `p2`.`product_id`
    FROM `product_images` AS `p2`
    WHERE (`p2`.`sort_order` = 1) AND `p2`.`sku_id` IS NULL
) AS `p3` ON `p`.`product_id` = `p3`.`product_id`
WHERE `w`.`shopper_reg_id` = @__shopperId_0
ORDER BY `w`.`wishlist_id` DESC
LIMIT @__p_2 OFFSET @__p_1

SELECT `w`.`wishlist_id` AS `WishlistId`, `p`.`product_id` AS `ProductId`, `p`.`product_name` AS `ProductName`, `w`.`sku_id` AS `SkuId`, `p`.`buscat_id` AS `Buscatid`, `p`.`prod_subcat_id` AS `prod_sub_catid`, COALESCE(`p1`.`file_name`, `p3`.`file_name`) AS `VariantImageUrl`, COALESCE(`s`.`discount_price`, `s`.`sku_cost`) AS `Price`, `b`.`bus_reg_id` AS `StoreId`, `b`.`business_name` AS `StoreName`, (`p`.`product_status` = 'Approved') AND `p`.`is_active` AS `IsProductAvailable`
FROM `wishlist` AS `w`
INNER JOIN `products` AS `p` ON `w`.`product_id` = `p`.`product_id`
INNER JOIN `business_registers` AS `b` ON `w`.`bus_reg_id` = `b`.`bus_reg_id`
INNER JOIN `sku_product_variants` AS `s` ON `w`.`sku_id` = `s`.`sku_id`
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT `p0`.`file_name`, `p0`.`sku_id`
    FROM `product_images` AS `p0`
    WHERE `p0`.`sort_order` = 1
) AS `p1` ON `w`.`sku_id` = `p1`.`sku_id`
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT `p2`.`file_name`, `p2`.`product_id`
    FROM `product_images` AS `p2`
    WHERE (`p2`.`sort_order` = 1) AND `p2`.`sku_id` IS NULL
) AS `p3` ON `p`.`product_id` = `p3`.`product_id`
WHERE `w`.`shopper_reg_id` = @__shopperId_0
ORDER BY `w`.`wishlist_id` DESC
LIMIT @__p_2 OFFSET @__p_1

        SELECT `p`.`payment_id` AS `PaymentId`, `p`.`payment_method` AS `PaymentMethod`, `p`.`amount_paid` AS `AmountPaid`, 1 AS `c`, `p`.`order_id`, ROW_NUMBER() OVER(PARTITION BY `p`.`order_id` ORDER BY `p`.`payment_date` DESC) AS `row`
        FROM `payments` AS `p`
    ) AS `p0`
    WHERE `p0`.`row` <= 1
) AS `p1` ON `s0`.`order_id` = `p1`.`order_id`

FROM `wishlist` AS `w`

INNER JOIN `business_registers` AS `b` ON `w`.`bus_reg_id` = `b`.`bus_reg_id`
INNER JOIN `sku_product_variants` AS `s` ON `w`.`sku_id` = `s`.`sku_id`
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT `p0`.`file_name`, `p0`.`sku_id`
    FROM `product_images` AS `p0`
    WHERE `p0`.`sort_order` = 1
) AS `p1` ON `w`.`sku_id` = `p1`.`sku_id`
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT `p2`.`file_name`, `p2`.`product_id`
    FROM `product_images` AS `p2`
    WHERE (`p2`.`sort_order` = 1) AND `p2`.`sku_id` IS NULL
) AS `p3` ON `p`.`product_id` = `p3`.`product_id`
WHERE `w`.`shopper_reg_id` = @__shopperId_0
ORDER BY `w`.`wishlist_id` DESC
LIMIT @__p_2 OFFSET @__p_1
